Theorem 105.1src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.siegel_lemma
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $\StablyUnramMACRoCZD {E}$. Let $J$ be a finite type. Let $u \in \GfMACRoFEM$. Let $T$ be a submodule of the set of functions from $J$ to $E$ over $E$. Let $s \in \Natural$ such that $\ModulefinrankMACRoFDO {E}{\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {T}}+ s = \FintypecardMACRoIR {J}$. Then there exists a matrix $X$ of $J$ and $E$, such that the kernel of $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVecLin}$ applied to the conjugate transpose of $\MatrixtransposeMACRoW {X}$ is equal to $T$, for all elements $i$ of $\FinMACRoFDI {s}$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SpacebaseLatticeMACRoCVO {(\SpacemoveMACRoCWW {(\stdSpaceMACRoEVB {E}{J})}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {1}{u})})}}{X (i)}$ and $X (i)\neq 0$ and $(\FinsetProd {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{i_{1}}{\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{Space\_\_archCoords\_\_app} (\SpacemoveMACRoCWW {(\stdSpaceMACRoEVB {E}{J})}{(\ProdmkMACRoDR {1}{u})}, X (i_{1}))}})^ {2 \times \numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E}}$ is less than or equal to the product of the exponentiation of the product of $8 \times (\numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E}\times s)$ and the root discriminant of $E$ to $\numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E}\times s$ and $\FillingsublatticeDiscMACRoAOS {\mathrm{Units\_\_val\_inv} (u)}{T}$.
Siegel's lemma: a subspace T of E^κ of codimension s is the orthogonal of s nonzero vectors X_i of E^κ ∩ uÔ^κ with (∏‖X_i‖)^(2f) ≤ (8fs·rd_E)^(fs)·d, ‖·‖ Euclidean over all f infinite places, d the discriminant `Filling.sublatticeDisc` of T ∩ uÔ^κ. Known result: Siegel's lemma over number fields (Bombieri–Vaaler).
Proof. By exists_orthonormal_mul, Space.vecAtPoint, ideleAbs_det_mkAdelicGL, Filling.map_conjTranspose, Space, Space.x, IsIntMatrix.mul, Filling.jacobi_perp_discriminant, zpow_sum_of_ne_zero, Filling.finAdeleMat_conjTranspose, U, finIdeleAbs, minkowski_second, Filling.finAdeleMat, mkAdelicGL, Filling.mul_eq_zero_of_range_le_ker, Space.archCoords, genIndex_eq_inv_finIdeleAbs, exists_mul_add_mul_eq, abs_norm_eq_prod_norm_sq, Space.move_vecAtPoint, U.gram_invariant, Filling.sublatticeBasis, Filling.sublatticeDisc, sum_norm_sq_mulVec_of_isometry, PlaceEmbedding.Φ, IsIntMatrix.mulVec
and 29 lemmas of Mathlib zpow_natCast, Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Eq.ge, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, le_mul_of_one_le_left, Matrix.fromRows_zero, Matrix.coe_units_inv, Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.fromBlocks_add, Eq.le, add_left_cancel, Prod.snd_sum, AddSubgroup.relIndex_eq_one, LinearMap.range_eq_top, zpow_comm, map_mul, le_antisymm, Matrix.mulVec_eq_sum, RingHom.map_det, mul_right_comm, Prod.fst_sum, Matrix.det_eq_zero_of_row_eq_zero, Matrix.map_mul, PiLp.norm_eq_of_L2, LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker, Fintype.sum_prod_type, Matrix.conjTranspose_mul, Real.sqrt_prod, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec
. ∎
Theorem 105.2src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.tsum_sublatticeDisc_inv_le
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$ and $\StablyUnramMACRoCZD {E}$. Let $J$ be a finite type. Let $u \in \GfMACRoFEM$. Let $s \in \Natural$. Then the sum of the function that maps $T$ to $\iteMACRoDD {\mathrm{Eq} (\ModulefinrankMACRoFDO {E}{\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {T}}+ s , \FintypecardMACRoIR {J})}{\mathrm{ENNReal\_\_ofReal} (\InvinvMACRoFW {((\FillingsublatticeDiscMACRoAOS {\mathrm{Units\_\_val\_inv} (u)}{T})^ {(\FintypecardMACRoIR {J}+ 1)})})}{0}$ and $\mathrm{SummationFilter\_\_unconditional}$ applied to the set of submodules of the set of functions from $J$ to $E$ over $E$ is less than or equal to $\mathrm{ENNReal\_\_ofReal}$ applied to the exponentiation of the product of $200$, $\numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E}$, the cardinality of $J$ and the root discriminant of $E$ to $\numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E}\times (\FintypecardMACRoIR {J}+ 1)\times s$.
For finite adelic unitary u let d(T) be the discriminant `Filling.sublatticeDisc` of T ∩ uÔ^κ. Over all subspaces T of E^κ of codimension s, ∑d(T)^(−(k+1)) ≤ (200fk·rd_E)^(f(k+1)s), where k = |κ| and f is the number of infinite places.
Proof. By stdSpace, Space.baseLattice, Space.move, Space.firstMinimum_le, tsum_inv_pow_le_of_separated, Filling.siegel_lemma, Space.one_le_firstMinimum, Filling.sublatticeDisc, tsum_prod_le_prod_tsum
and 21 lemmas of Mathlib div_le_of_le_mul₀, Summable.mul_right, mul_assoc, Finset.one_le_prod₀, NeZero.one_le, ENNReal.ofReal_tsum_of_nonneg, Fin.prod_const, Finset.prod_const, ENNReal.tsum_mul_right, pow_mul, tsum_mul_right, ENNReal.le_tsum, ENNReal.ofReal_prod_of_nonneg, one_div, mul_add, ENNReal.tsum_fiberwise, LE.le.trans, Finset.prod_pow, mul_pow, one_pos, ENNReal.ofReal_pow
. ∎

Theorem 105.4src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: besselU_dense
Let $E$ be a field such that $E$ is a number field and $\NumberFieldIsCMFieldMACRoFDC {E}$. Let $x_{1}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{1}}{E}$ such that $T$ is a hermitian unit. Let $T' \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{1}}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$ such that the map of $\ShapeFormsplitGramMACRoCQR {T}$ and the algebra map of $E$ and $\udfMACRoFEH {E}$ is equal to $\ShapeFormsplitGramMACRoCQR {T'}$. Then $\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO \leq \mathrm{Subgroup\_\_topologicalClosure} (\mathrm{Subgroup\_\_map} (\UmapMACRoDFC {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_algebraMap} (E , \udfMACRoFEH {E})}{\mathrm{of\_eq\_true} (\mathrm{Eq\_\_trans} (\mathrm{forall\_congr} (\lambda x_{5} . \mathrm{Eq\_\_trans} (\mathrm{congrArg} (\lambda x_{6} . \EqtMACRoDE {\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (x_{5})}}{x_{6}}, \mathrm{star\_algebraMap\_finAdele} (x_{5})), \mathrm{eq\_self} (\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (x_{5})}))), \mathrm{implies\_true} (E)))}{e}, \ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO))$.
Strong approximation for the unipotent group 𝖴 of the form H^ϱ ⊥ T, with T invertible hermitian over E: every open set of matrices containing a finite adelic point of 𝖴 contains a rational point of 𝖴.
Proof. By unipotentBelow_le_besselU, Shape.Form.splitGram, eichlerMap, U.map, skewPart, TraceOne, heisenbergElem_mem_floorUnipotent, exists_eq_heisenbergElem_of_mem, Shape.ϱ, U.isClosedEmbedding_mat, U, IsHermUnit.isUnit, IsHermUnit, denseRange_algebraMap_finAdele, IsHermUnit.conjTranspose_eq, entrywise, TraceOne.heisenbergElem, unipotentBelow_eq_besselU, unipotentBelow_induction, TraceOne.τ, inclMatrix, eq_one_of_mem_floorGroup_inf
and 18 lemmas of Mathlib IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum.adicCompletion.ext, add_mul, Eq.ge, DenseRange.prodMap, map_mem_closure, Function.Surjective.denseRange, DenseRange.piMap, Units.ext, Matrix.IsHermitian.map, le_or_gt, IsDedekindDomain.FiniteAdeleRing.ext, IsUnit.map, DenseRange.comp, LE.le.trans, Continuous.star, Topology.IsEmbedding.continuous_iff, Matrix.ext, MulMemClass.mul_mem
. ∎

Theorem 105.6src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.exists_besselU_approx
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$. Let $x_{3} \in \SubtypeSet {x_{16}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillinglatMiddleMACRoANI {S}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}})}}{x_{16}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO}{x_{3}}$. Let $y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{13}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillinglatMiddleMACRoANI {S}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}})}}{x_{13}})}$. Then there exists an element $u$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{12}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E})}}{x_{12}})}$, such that $u$ is an element of $\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO$ and $\InvinvMACRoFW {y}\times (\InvinvMACRoFW {x_{3}}\times \mathrm{Filling\_\_ratToFin\_\_app} (S , u))\times y$ is an element of $\FillingprincipalLevelMACRoANY {S}$.
Strong approximation for 𝖴: for x ∈ 𝖴(𝔸_f) and any y in the finite adelic unitary group, some rational u ∈ 𝖴 has y⁻¹ x⁻¹ u y ∈ K(g).
Proof. By Filling.principalLevel, IsOpen.preimage_of_continuous, Filling.toLatBasis, Subgroup.IsOpen, Filling.principalLevel_eq_unitaryLevel, isOpen_Ô, besselU_dense, Filling.map_splitGram_ratMiddle, Filling.toLatBasis_mem_besselU, isOpen_intGL
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib Continuous.subtype_mk, IsOpen.preimage_val, IsOpen.and, Set.exists_mem_image, IsOpen.matrix, closure_inter_open_nonempty_iff
. ∎
Theorem 105.7src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.unipotentChar_eq_of_mem_principalLevel
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$. Let $u \in \SubtypeSet {x_{15}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillinglatMiddleMACRoANI {S}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}})}}{x_{15}})}$. Let $v \in \SubtypeSet {x_{14}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillinglatMiddleMACRoANI {S}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}})}}{x_{14}})}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO}{u}$. Assume that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO}{v}$. Let $g \in \SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\UMACRoFEQ {1}}{x_{9}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\FillingprincipalLevelMACRoANY {S}}{\InvinvMACRoFW {u}\times (\InvinvMACRoFW {\mathrm{Filling\_\_inclHFin\_\_app} (S , g)}\times v \times \mathrm{Filling\_\_inclHFin\_\_app} (S , g))}$. Then $\FillingunipotentCharMACRoAPF {S}{v}$ is equal to $\FillingunipotentCharMACRoAPF {S}{u}$.
If u, v ∈ 𝖴 and h ∈ H are finite adelic points with u⁻¹ h⁻¹ v h ∈ K(g), then the character `Filling.unipotentChar` takes the same value at u and v.
Proof. By Filling.inclHFin_mem_besselH, inv_mul_simpleRootSum_mem, mem_unitriGroup_of_mem_besselU, Filling.selfDualChar_eq_one_of_mem, simpleRootSum_mul, Filling.selfDualChar, Filling.principalLevel_eq_unitaryLevel, mem_unitriGroup_of_mem_floorGroup, Filling.unipotentChar
and 7 lemmas of Mathlib AddChar.map_add_eq_mul, Eq.le, InvMemClass.inv_mem, mul_add, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel_left, MulMemClass.mul_mem
. ∎
Theorem 105.8src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.eq_and_mem_classUnip_of_besselS
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$ such that $\FillingIsAdmissibleMACRoAKZ {S}$. Let $c , d \in \FillingClassesMACRoAKG {S}$. Let $\gamma \in \SubtypeSet {x_{9}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {(\FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E})}}{x_{9}})}$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormbesselSMACRoCQN}{\gamma}$ and $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\FillingprincipalLevelMACRoANY {S}}{\FillingtermPositionMACRoAOX {S}{c}{d}{\gamma}}$. Then $c$ is equal to $d$ and $\gamma$ is an element of $\FillingclassUnipMACRoAMJ {S}{c}$.
Under the standing hypotheses, a rational element γ of the Bessel subgroup S that is a term over (c, 1, c') forces c = c' and lies in 𝖴_c(g).
Proof. By Filling.inclHFin_mem_besselH, Filling.aut_columnLevel, eq_and_eq_one_of_doubleCoset_rep, exists_besselU_mul_toBesselH, Filling.h, Filling.classUnip, Filling.principalLevel_eq_unitaryLevel, Membership.mem.termPosition_mem_besselU, mem_unitaryLevel_of_besselU_mul
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib Eq.le, map_eq_one_iff, Quotient.out_eq, Fact.out
. ∎

Theorem 105.11src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.eq_one_of_reduceAt_eq_one
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$. Assume that $\StablyUnramMACRoCZD {E}$. Let $n$ be a finite type such that $\FintypecardMACRoIR {n}\leq \ShapekMACRoCRP {x_{2}}$. Let $i \in \FinMACRoFDI {2}$. Let $\gamma \in \SubtypeSet {x_{11}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\UMACRoFEQ {\SpaceJMACRoCTK {(\stdSpaceMACRoEVB {E}{n})}}}{x_{11}})}$. Let $g \in \GfMACRoFEM$ such that $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\KfMACRoBQE {E}{n}}{\InvinvMACRoFW {g}\times \mathrm{Space\_\_embFin\_\_app} (\stdSpaceMACRoEVB {E}{n}, \gamma)\times g}$ and for all elements $w$ of $\FillingchosenPlacesMACRoAMC {S}{i}$, $\mathrm{Kf\_\_reduceAt\_\_app} (w , \mathrm{times} (\InvinvMACRoFW {g}\times \mathrm{Space\_\_embFin\_\_app} (\stdSpaceMACRoEVB {E}{n}, \gamma), g , \mathrm{hk}))$ is equal to $1$. Then $\gamma$ is equal to $1$.
Freeness: for rank n ≤ |κ|, a rational element γ of the standard unitary group U(n) is 1 if, for some finite adelic g, g⁻¹γg is integral and congruent to 1 modulo every chosen place above the prime q_i.
Proof. By Filling.ϱ_le_k, Filling.le_levelPrime, classAut_eq_bot, Filling.card_chosenPlaces, finrank_eq_two_mul_card_infinitePlace, Filling.chosenPlaces_q, Filling.k_le_b
and 5 lemmas of Mathlib le_rfl, Eq.le, pow_mul, map_eq_one_iff, Fact.out
. ∎
Theorem 105.12src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.frameImage_mul
Let $R$ be a commutative ring such that $R$ is a star ring. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x_{2}}{R}$. Let $s \in \SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{7}})}$. Let $s' \in \SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{6}})}$. Then $\frameImageMACRoDWD {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {(s \times s')}}$ is equal to $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {s}\times \frameImageMACRoDWD {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {s'}}$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Theorem 105.18src/New/Filling/NearTerms.lean: Filling.norm_sq_middle_part_eq
Let $x$ be a shape. Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x}$. Let $\gamma \in \SubtypeSet {x_{11}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\FillingGMACRoAKT {S}{E})}{x_{11}})}$. Let $y$ be a function from $\FinMACRoFDI {(\ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}+ 1)}$ to $E$. Let $D \in E$. Let $t$ be a function from $\ShapeBesselIndexMACRoCQI {x}$ to $E$. Assume that $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVec} (y)$ is equal to $\mathrm{Pi\_\_single} (\ShapetopMACRoCRX {x}, D)$. Assume that $\mathrm{Matrix\_\_mulVec} (y)$ is equal to $t$. Theorem 105.38src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.liouville_gram
Let $E$ be a field such that $\CMTypeMACRoFEG {E}$. Let $S$ and $T$ be finite types. Assume that $\StablyUnramMACRoCZD {E}$. Let $Q$ be a matrix of $T$ and $E$ such that for all infinite places $w$ of $E$, the map of $Q$ and $\PlaceEmbeddinguaMACRoCKZ {w}$ is a positive part. Let $a$ and $b$ be matrixes of $S$ and $E$. Let $v , v' \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {S}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Assume that $\MatrixdetMACRoGY {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (a)\times Q \times b)}\neq 0$. Let $x_{4} \in \Real$ such that $0 \leq x_{4}$ and if $\MatrixdetMACRoGY {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v})\times (\FillingfinAdeleMatMACRoAMR {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (a)\times Q \times b)}\times \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v'}))}$ is a unit, then $x_{4} \leq \mathrm{genIndex\_\_app} (\MatrixdetMACRoGY {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v})\times (\FillingfinAdeleMatMACRoAMR {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (a)\times Q \times b)}\times \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v'}))})$. Then $x_{4} ^ {2}\leq \mathrm{genIndex\_\_app} (\MatrixdetMACRoGY {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v}})^ {2}\times (\absMACRoP {\RatcastMACRoFDW {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_norm\_\_app} (\Rational , \MatrixdetMACRoGY {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (a)\times Q \times a)})}}\times \absMACRoP {\RatcastMACRoFDW {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_norm\_\_app} (\Rational , \MatrixdetMACRoGY {(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (b)\times Q \times b)})}}\times \mathrm{genIndex\_\_app} (\MatrixdetMACRoGY {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {v'}})^ {2})$.
Liouville inequality for Gram determinants: let Q be totally positive definite, det(aᴴQb) ≠ 0, v, v' invertible finite adelic. If x ≥ 0 and x ≤ [det(vᴴaᴴQbv')], then x² ≤ [det v]²·|N det(aᴴQa)|·|N det(bᴴQb)|·[det v']², N the norm to ℚ, [·] = `genIndex`.
Proof. By norm_det_sq_le_of_posSemidef, Filling.map_conjTranspose, Filling.finAdeleMat, genIndex, abs_norm_eq_prod_norm_sq
and 9 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.prod_mul_distrib, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.fromBlocks_toBlocks, Matrix.PosSemidef.conjTranspose_mul_mul_same, sq_le_sq, RingHom.map_det, Matrix.PosDef.posSemidef, Finset.prod_pow, mul_pow
. ∎
Definition 105.39src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.linkMetric
Let $E$ be a set. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$. Then $\FillinglinkMetricMACRoANP {S}$ is a matrix of $\mathrm{Sum} (\ShapeMiddleIndexMACRoCQX {x_{2}}, \ShapeMiddleIndexMACRoCQX {x_{2}})$ and $E$ defined as $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\InvinvMACRoFW {2}}{\mathrm{Matrix\_\_fromBlocks} (\FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E}, 0 , 0 , \FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E})}+ \HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\InvinvMACRoFW {\FillingNMACRoALI {S}}}{(\mathrm{Matrix\_\_conjTranspose} (\FillingzFormPairMACRoAPJ {(\FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E})})\times \FillingzFormPairMACRoAPJ {(\FillingratMiddleMACRoAOB {S}{E})})}$.

Definition 105.40src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.linkDiscSq
Let $E$ and $X$ be sets. Let $x_{2}$ be a shape. Let $S \in \FillingMACRoAKC {E}{x_{2}}$. Let $v$ be a matrix of $X$ and $E$. Let $e \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {X}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Theorem 105.58src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.tsum_le_of_encard_range_le
Let $X$ and $Y$ be sets. Let $A \in \wp X$. Let $R$ be a function from elements of $X$ and elements of $X$ to propositions. Let $N , x \in \ENNRealMACRoCG$. Let $w$ be a function from $X$ to $\ENNRealMACRoCG$. Let $\phi$ be a function from elements of $A$ to elements of $Y$ such that $\mathrm{ENat\_\_toENNReal}$ applied to the cardinality in the range of $\phi$ is less than or equal to $N$ and for all elements $a_{3}$ and $b_{1}$ of $A$, if $\phi (a_{3})$ is equal to $\phi (b_{1})$, then $R (a_{3} , b_{1})$. Assume that for all elements $B$ of $\wp X$, if $B$ is a subset of $A$, then for all elements $x_{2}$ of $X$, if $x_{2}$ is an element of $B$, then if for all elements $a_{2}$ of $X$, if $a_{2}$ is an element of $B$, then for all elements $b$ of $X$, if $b$ is an element of $B$, then $R (a_{2} , b)$, then the sum of the function that maps $a_{1}$ to $w (a_{1})$ and $\mathrm{SummationFilter\_\_unconditional}$ applied to the set of elements of $B$ is less than or equal to $x$. Then the sum of the function that maps $a$ to $w (a)$ and $\mathrm{SummationFilter\_\_unconditional}$ applied to the set of elements of $A$ is less than or equal to $N \times x$.
Colour A with at most N colours so that elements of one colour are R-related. If every nonempty subset of A of pairwise R-related elements has total weight at most x, then A has total weight at most Nx.
Proof.
By 4 lemmas of Mathlib ENNReal.tsum_set_const, ENNReal.tsum_le_tsum, tsum_image, ENNReal.tsum_fiberwise
. ∎
Theorem 105.59src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.tsum_le_of_colouring
Let $X$ be a set. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $A \in \wp X$. Let $Y$ be an additive commutative group. Let $f \in X \rightarrow I \rightarrow Y$. Let $K$ and $K'$ be subgroups of $Y$ such that the relative index of $K$ and $K'$ is distinct from $0$ and for all elements $a_{3}$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {A}{a_{3}}$, then for all elements $i_{1}$ of $I$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {K'}{f (a_{3} , i_{1})}$. Let $w$ be a function from $X$ to $\ENNRealMACRoCG$. Let $x_{2} \in \ENNRealMACRoCG$ such that for all elements $B$ of $\wp X$, if $B$ is a subset of $A$, then for all elements $x_{4}$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {B}{x_{4}}$, then if for all elements $a_{2}$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {B}{a_{2}}$, then for all elements $b$ of $X$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {B}{b}$, then for all elements $i$ of $I$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {K}{f (a_{2} , i)- f (b , i)}$, then the sum of the function that maps $a_{1}$ to $w (a_{1})$ and $\mathrm{SummationFilter\_\_unconditional}$ applied to the set of elements of $B$ is less than or equal to $x_{2}$. Then the sum of the function that maps $a$ to $w (a)$ and $\mathrm{SummationFilter\_\_unconditional}$ applied to the set of elements of $A$ is less than or equal to the product of $\mathrm{ENNReal\_\_ofReal}$ applied to the exponentiation of the relative index of $K$ and $K'$ to $\FintypecardMACRoIR {I}$ and $x_{2}$.
Proof. By Filling.tsum_le_of_encard_range_le
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib QuotientAddGroup.eq_iff_sub_mem, Set.encard_le_card, AddSubgroup.mem_addSubgroupOf
. ∎
Theorem 105.60src/New/Filling/BoxPartition.lean: Filling.encard_integral_le
Let $X$ be a set. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $E'$ be a field. Let $A \in \wp X$. Assume that $E'$ is a number field. Assume that $\PlaceEmbeddingMACRoCKY {E'}$. Let $r \in \Real$. Let $f \in X \rightarrow I \rightarrow E'$. Assume that $0 \leq r$. Assume that for all elements $a$ of $X$, if $a$ is an element of $A$, then for all elements $i$ of $I$, $f (a , i)$ is integral and for all infinite places $v$ of $E'$, $\NormnormMACRoAK {\mathrm{PlaceEmbedding\_\_\_u3a6\_\_\_app} (v , f (a , i))}\leq r$. Assume that $f$ is injective on $A$. Then $\mathrm{ENat\_\_toENNReal}$ applied to the cardinality in $A$ is less than or equal to $\mathrm{ENNReal\_\_ofReal} ((1 + 2 \times r)^ {\FintypecardMACRoIR {I}\times \numInfPlacesMACRoEIW {E'}\times 2})$.
If f maps A injectively to vectors in E^ι whose entries are algebraic integers of absolute value at most r at every infinite place, then A has at most (1 + 2r)^(2|ι|m) elements, m the number of infinite places.
Proof. By eq_zero_of_norm_Φ, encard_le_of_separated, PlaceEmbedding.Φ
and 7 lemmas of Mathlib Function.mt, pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonneg, eq_of_sub_eq_zero, IsIntegral.sub, pi_norm_lt_iff, not_lt, one_pos
. ∎

Definition 105.129src/New/Filling/TopFloor.lean: IntegralPlace.phase
Let $A$ be a set. Let $x$ be a shape. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x}{A}$. Let $g$ be a unit of $A$. Let $z \in \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{3}})}$. Then $\IntegralPlacephaseMACRoBDN {g}{z}$ is a complex defined as $\mathrm{dite}$ applied to the proposition that there exists an element $u$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{8}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{8}})}$, such that $u$ is an element of $\ShapeFormbesselUMACRoCQO$ and $\InvinvMACRoFW {u}\times z$ is an element of $\unitaryLevelMACRoEZQ {\IntegralPlaceRMACRoBDJ}{g}{\ShapeFormsplitGramMACRoCQR {T}}$, the function that maps $h$ to $\mathrm{Place\_\_\_u3c8\_\_\_app} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {g}}\times \simpleRootSumMACRoETC {\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (h)})$ and the function that maps $h_{1}$ to $0$.
The function χ_g equal to ψ(σ(u)/g) on uK(g), for a chosen u in 𝖴, and to 0 off 𝖴K(g); σ(u) sums the entries at the simple roots, 𝖴 = `besselU`, K(g) = `unitaryLevel` R g.

. ∎

Theorem 105.160src/New/Filling/TopFloor.lean: IntegralPlace.IsStdLevel.exists_gauge_depth_of_ne_zero
Let $A$ be a star ring. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $x$ be a shape. Assume that $\SemilocalFieldMACRoCPY {A}$. Assume that $\IntegralPlaceMACRoBDD {A}$. Let $m , j , K \in \Natural$. Let $T \in \ShapeFormMACRoCQJ {x}{A}$ such that $\ShapeFormsplitGramMACRoCQR {T}$ is a hermitian unit. Let $F \in \LevelMACRoBRH {I}{T}{(m + 1)}$. Let $Z \in \LevelIntermediateGroupMACRoBSE {F}$. Assume that $\IntegralPlaceIsStdLevelMACRoBDF {F}$. Assume that $j \leq \ShapeufMACRoFEK {x}$. Let $p$ and $q$ be functions from $\Natural$ to $\UnitsMACRoEL {A}$ such that there exists an element $x_{4}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{6}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\ShapeFormGMACRoCQK {T}}{x_{6}})}$, such that $\LevelformMajorantMACRoBTE {j}{p}{q}{\LevelIntermediateGroupbesselVectorpartMACRoGPH {Z}}{\LevelIntermediateGroupbesselVectorpartMACRoGPH {Z}}{x_{4}}\neq 0$. Then there exists an element $\alpha$ of $\UnitsMACRoEL {A}$, such that there exists an element $a$ of $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\IntegralPlaceRMACRoBDJ}{\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\alpha}}$, such that there exists an element $b$ of $\mathrm{Eqt}$ applied to the star of $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\alpha}$ and $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\alpha}$, such that $\SizeIsHeadMACRoCSO {j}{p}$, $\SizeIsHeadMACRoCSO {j}{q}$, if $j \neq 0$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\GaugetoSizeMACRoASG {(\IntegralPlaceIsStdLevelgaugeMACRoBDG {\alpha}{a}{b})}}{1}}{\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {\LevelgMACRoBTG {F}}}}$, for all $i < j$ in $\Natural$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\SizeofSizeMACRoCSR {\GaugetoSizeMACRoASG {(\IntegralPlaceIsStdLevelgaugeMACRoBDG {\alpha}{a}{b})}}{1}}{\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {p (i)}}- \UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\InvinvMACRoFW {q (i)}}}$ and for all elements $E$ of $\Natural$, if $4 \times E \leq K$, then $\GaugeubMACRoARV {(\IntegralPlaceIsStdLevelgaugeMACRoBDG {\alpha}{a}{b})}^ {E}\leq (\frac{ 2}{\mathrm{Place\_\_md\_\_app} (\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\LevelgMACRoBTG {F}})})^ {K}\times \mathrm{ENNReal\_\_ofReal} (\finHeadWeightMACRoDUK {K}{j}{p}\times \finHeadWeightMACRoDUK {K}{j}{q})$.
If `Level.formMajorant` j p q of W_Z is not identically 0, j ≤ ϱ, then in some gauge of depth α: `Size.IsHead` holds for p, q; 1/g (if j ≠ 0) and 1/p_i − 1/q_i (i < j) have size 1; Λ^E ≤ (2/md g)^K·Λf(p)Λf(q) whenever 4E ≤ K (`finHeadWeight` K j).

Theorem 105.189src/New/Filling/AmplitudeSums.lean: Filling.amplitudeSum_besselS_one
